# ETAM Stage 2 results

Snapshot: **2026-11-10 (Asia/Jerusalem)**. This is an incomplete campaign snapshot,
read from saved result payloads and task statuses, rather than the older campaign
`RESULTS.md` harvests. It includes 39 completed ETAM-2 MBD pretraining runs
(13 arms × 3 seeds), eight Stage 1 re-evaluations, 24 strict-baseline cells,
six matched-exposure baseline cells, three Stage 1 Synthea evaluations and the
MBD/public-store preparation outputs.

[Methodology](methodology.md) · [Positives](positives.md) ·
[Negatives](negatives.md) · [Analysis](analysis.md) ·
[Stage 1 results](../results.md)

Regenerate the tables and complete numeric appendix from the repository root:

```bash
conda run -n KBC python docs/etam/make_stage2_tables.py
```

Source roots are `/groups/bshapira_group/guyhada/campaigns/etam2_mbd_20261108`,
`etam2_transfer_20261108` and `etam2_cpuprep_20261108` under the same campaign directory.
The pilot manifest is included for status; it has no completed result payloads.
The common tables keep planned arms visible, and the expandable appendix contains
all finite numeric fields, including diagnostics omitted from the compact tables.

## Findings at this snapshot

- The reference has test joint NLL **2.5094 ± 0.0045** and next-type accuracy
  **0.5962 ± 0.0009** across three independent pretraining seeds.
- Dual decoding improves joint NLL over independent heads (**2.6606 ± 0.0056**),
  with a next-type accuracy improvement of **0.0018**.
- Reference strict frozen-probe AUC is **0.7204 ± 0.0059**, compared with
  **0.7151 ± 0.0002** for the best completed strict baseline (pool PRAGMA-MLM).
  The gap does **not** exceed the reference spread.
- Time point error and PIT calibration are worse than the single-seed Stage 1 M10
  re-evaluation: RMSE **2.357 ± 0.029** versus **1.492**, PIT KS
  **0.0448 ± 0.0034** versus **0.0055**. Units are log1p seconds for RMSE.
- No completed ETAM fine-tunes, data-scaling runs, external-baseline runs,
  label-efficiency jobs, simulations or common-device latency job are available.
  ETAM-2 transfer runs have no completed result; Stage 1 Stack Overflow failed.

Generated 2026-11-10 18:27 IST by `make_stage2_tables.py` from `/groups/bshapira_group/guyhada/campaigns/etam2_mbd_20261108/runs`. Cells: mean ± sd over pretraining seeds (`(n/planned)` while incomplete); `(Δ)` against `ref`, `*` beyond the larger sd. Test numbers use the benchmark's examples; `valid` is 10,000 validation users. Missing metrics are pending or not applicable, not zero. Stars indicate magnitude, not significance or improvement; coverage is assessed against its nominal level. Stage 1 legacy arms have one pretraining seed; 20-epoch and HPO arms have a larger budget than M10. Parameter/FLOP counts alone do not establish matched compute.
